{"forms":[{"form":"dials in","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"dialing in","tags":["US","participle","present"]},{"form":"dialling in","tags":["UK","participle","present"]},{"form":"dialed in","tags":["US","participle","past"]},{"form":"dialed in","tags":["US","past"]},{"form":"dialled in","tags":["UK","participle","past"]},{"form":"dialled in","tags":["UK","past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Before starting the recording, make sure you've dialled in the right synthesiser settings.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To configure or set up (particularly a complex machine with many configuration options [especially knobs and dials)."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I tried dialling in to the server, but I couldn't connect.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To connect (to a system or service) by telephone."]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"dial out"}],"examples":[{"text":"Did you dial in to that earnings announcement?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To join or participate in (a conference call)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"call in"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"The company quickly dialled in to the fact that customers wanted greater convenience.","type":"example"},{"text":"Nowadays most people are dialled in to the idea that smoking is bad for you.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To apprehend and align oneself (to an idea, circumstance, way of thinking, etc.)."],"tags":["figuratively"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He was pushy, but he dialed that in until they found him tolerable.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To reduce (something) to an appropriate level."],"tags":["figuratively"]},{"glosses":["To achieve a perfect result in brewing coffee by manipulating grind size, water amount, etc."],"topics":["cooking","food","lifestyle"]}],"upstream_line":2262600,"upstream_offset":7029100900,"upstream_record_sha256":"8e420590c96b68bf7e8283efc19c41bf0b4f64bcc3c74e9debf0c953447f72c4","word":"dial in"}

{"antonyms":[{"word":"activity"}],"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"rests","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I need to get a good rest tonight; I was up late last night.","type":"example"},{"text":"The sun sets, and the workers go to their rest.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Relief from work or activity by sleeping; sleep."],"raw_tags":["of a person or animal"],"synonyms":[{"word":"sleep"},{"word":"slumber"}],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"We took a rest at the top of the hill to get our breath back.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any relief from exertion; a state of quiet and relaxation."],"synonyms":[{"word":"break"},{"word":"repose"},{"word":"time off"}],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"It was nice to have a rest from the phone ringing when I unplugged it for a while.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Peace; freedom from worry, anxiety, annoyances; tranquility."],"synonyms":[{"word":"peace"},{"word":"quiet"},{"word":"roo"},{"word":"silence"},{"word":"stillness"},{"word":"tranquility"}],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The boulder came to rest just behind the house after rolling down the mountain.","type":"example"},{"text":"The ocean was finally at rest.","type":"example"},{"text":"Now that we're all in agreement, we can put that issue to rest.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A state of inactivity; a state of little or no motion; a state of completion."],"raw_tags":["of an object or concept"],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"She was laid to rest in the village cemetery.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A final position after death. Also, death itself: \"Not alone, not alone would I go to my rest in the heart of the love...\" -- George William Russell (\"Love\")"],"synonyms":[{"word":"peace"}],"tags":["euphemistic","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Remember there's a rest at the end of the fourth bar.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A pause of a specified length in a piece of music."],"tags":["countable"],"topics":["entertainment","lifestyle","music"]},{"glosses":["A written symbol indicating such a pause in a musical score such as in sheet music."],"tags":["countable"],"topics":["entertainment","lifestyle","music"]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"motion"}],"examples":[{"text":"The body's centre of gravity may affect its state of rest.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Absence of motion."],"tags":["uncountable"],"topics":["natural-sciences","physical-sciences","physics"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Higgins can't quite reach the white with his cue, so he'll be using the rest.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A stick with a U-, V- or X-shaped head used to support the tip of a cue when the cue ball is otherwise out of reach."],"tags":["countable"],"topics":["ball-games","games","hobbies","lifestyle","snooker","sports"]},{"examples":[{"text":"She put the phone receiver back in its rest.","type":"example"},{"text":"He placed his hands on the arm rests of the chair.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any object designed to be used to support something else."],"synonyms":[{"word":"cradle"},{"word":"support"}],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["A projection from the right side of the cuirass of armour, serving to support the lance."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A place where one may rest, either temporarily, as in an inn, or permanently, as, in an abode."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A short pause in reading poetry;
